- **call-insights**: Claude reads a matched Fathom recording and lists what the prospect said (objections, struggles, goals, income), how the call ended and whether the prospect attended. Code checks every quote against the transcript, Jev checks each item, and the result shows as a "What they said" card on the meeting, a suggested result that prefills the outcome dialogs, and report rows. The plan is `docs/reviews/ca ([MagnusVA/magnus-crm](https://github.com/MagnusVA/magnus-crm/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/call-insights.md))
- **call-intel-collector**: Finds a customer's recorded calls in Gong and Fathom (sales, discovery, technical, negotiation, handoff) and extracts onboarding facts with verbatim quotes and links. - **call-miner**: Use this subagent to extract the six required fields (Trigger, Incumbent, Pain language, Buyer profile, Features, Company shape) from a batch of call transcripts in parallel. Spawn one per ~10-25 calls when the corpus is large. Each subagent writes its own per-call markdown files and reports back paths only — not the extractions themselves — to keep the parent agent's context clean. ([anisbennaceur1/icp-refiner](https://github.com/anisbennaceur1/icp-refiner/blob/HEAD/agents/call-miner.md) · ★3)

- **Cami Cenários**: 1. Todo cenário precisa responder a um risco ou requisito real. 2. Duplicidade de cenário confunde cobertura e revisão. 3. - **camstack-implementer**: Implements an APPROVED, written plan in camstack-server — server, system, types, sdk, addons, capabilities — with TDD and the repo's gates. Use for any pure implementation step once design and plan are settled; the coordinating session keeps understanding, design, planning and review. Not for UI (camstack-ui-engineer), not for live diagnosis (camstack-diagnostician), never ships. ([camstack/server](https://github.com/camstack/server/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/camstack-implementer.md) · ★1)
- **camstack-reviewer**: Reviews a finished CamStack change BEFORE it lands on main — a worker's commits in a worktree, or a range on main. Judges the diff against what existed before it, not against the plan that produced it: inventories the behaviour the old code offered per case and proves each piece survived, checks comments and docs against the code, checks that the specs would fail without the fix, and checks the AGENTS.md rules. Use after every implementer / ui-engineer / test-fixer run and before merge. Read-only: it never edits, commits, deploys or calls the cluster. ([camstack/server](https://github.com/camstack/server/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/camstack-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **camstack-test-fixer**: Makes CI green on camstack-server — a red CI run on main, a failing spec, a flaky (timing, order or host dependent) test, a TypeScript 7 typecheck error over the pins. Use when CI is red or a test fails intermittently; it classifies flaky vs real, fixes the root cause deterministically and proves it. Never weakens, skips or retries a test. ([camstack/server](https://github.com/camstack/server/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/camstack-test-fixer.md) · ★1)

Conservative by design: consistency with the library and tokens over visual novelty; viewer layout changes are proposed to the operator, never made unasked. ([camstack/server](https://github.com/camstack/server/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/camstack-ui-engineer.md) · ★1)
